    39→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
    42→	PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution `json:"prompt_attributions,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// PendingPromptAttribution holds attribution calculated at prompt start (before agent runs).
    45→	// This is moved to PromptAttributions when SaveChanges is called.
    46→	PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution `json:"pending_prompt_attribution,omitempty"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt,
    50→// calculated BEFORE the agent runs. This allows us to separate user edits (made between
    51→// prompts) from agent edits (made during prompt execution).
    52→//
    53→// Fields:
    54→//   - CheckpointNumber: Which checkpoint this attribution is for (1-indexed)
    55→//   - UserLinesAdded/Removed: User edits since the last checkpoint (lastCheckpoint → worktree)
    56→//   - AgentLinesAdded/Removed: Cumulative agent work so far (base → lastCheckpoint)
    57→//   - UserAddedPerFile: Per-file breakdown of user additions (for accurate modification tracking)
    58→//
    59→// Note: For checkpoint 1, AgentLinesAdded/Removed are always 0 because there is no previous
    60→// checkpoint to compare against. This doesn't mean the agent hasn't done work yet - it means
    61→// we can't measure cumulative agent work until after the first checkpoint is created.
    62→// These fields become meaningful starting from checkpoint 2.
    63→//
    64→// UserAddedPerFile enables accurate tracking of user self-modifications. When a user modifies
    65→// their own previously-added lines (not agent lines), we shouldn't subtract from the agent's
    66→// contribution. See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
    67→type PromptAttribution struct {
    68→	CheckpointNumber  int            `json:"checkpoint_number"`
    69→	UserLinesAdded    int            `json:"user_lines_added"`
    70→	UserLinesRemoved  int            `json:"user_lines_removed"`
    71→	AgentLinesAdded   int            `json:"agent_lines_added"`             // Always 0 for checkpoint 1 (no previous checkpoint)
    72→	AgentLinesRemoved int            `json:"agent_lines_removed"`           // Always 0 for checkpoint 1 (no previous checkpoint)
    73→	UserAddedPerFile  map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"` // Per-file user additions for modification tracking
    74→}
    75→
    76→// CheckpointInfo represents checkpoint metadata stored on the sessions branch.
    77→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
    78→type CheckpointInfo struct {
    79→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID `json:"checkpoint_id"` // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    80→	SessionID        string          `json:"session_id"`
    81→	CreatedAt        time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
    82→	CheckpointsCount int             `json:"checkpoints_count"`
    83→	FilesTouched     []string        `json:"files_touched"`
    84→	Agent            agent.AgentType `json:"agent,omitempty"` // Human-readable agent name (e.g., "Claude Code")
    85→	IsTask           bool            `json:"is_task,omitempty"`
    86→	ToolUseID        string          `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
    87→	SessionCount     int             `json:"session_count,omitempty"` // Number of sessions (1 if omitted)
    88→	SessionIDs       []string        `json:"session_ids,omitempty"`   // All session IDs in this checkpoint
    89→}
    90→
    91→// CondenseResult contains the result of a session condensation operation.
    92→type CondenseResult struct {
    93→	CheckpointID         id.CheckpointID // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    94→	SessionID            string
    95→	CheckpointsCount     int
    96→	FilesTouched         []string
    97→	TotalTranscriptLines int // Total lines in transcript after this condensation
    98→}
    99→
   100→// ExtractedSessionData contains data extracted from a shadow branch.
   101→type ExtractedSessionData struct {
   102→	Transcript          []byte   // Full transcript content for the session

   104→	Prompts             []string // All user prompts from this portion
   105→	Context             []byte   // Generated context.md content
   106→	FilesTouched        []string
   107→	TokenUsage          *agent.TokenUsage // Token usage calculated from transcript (since TranscriptLinesAtStart)
   108→}

     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"time"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"

    15→
    16→// Shadow strategy session state methods.
    17→// Uses session.StateStore for persistence.
    18→
    19→// loadSessionState loads session state using the StateStore.
    20→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    21→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return nil, err
    24→	}
    25→	state, err := store.Load(context.Background(), sessionID)
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→	return sessionStateToStrategy(state), nil
    30→}
    31→
    32→// saveSessionState saves session state using the StateStore.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) saveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    34→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return err
    37→	}
    38→	if err := store.Save(context.Background(), sessionStateFromStrategy(state)); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// clearSessionState clears session state using the StateStore.
    45→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) clearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
    46→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→	if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	return nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// listAllSessionStates returns all active session states.
    57→// It filters out orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    58→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    59→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    70→		return nil, nil
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var states []*SessionState
    79→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    80→		state := sessionStateToStrategy(sessionState)
    81→
    82→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists
    83→		// Only cleanup if the session has created checkpoints (CheckpointCount > 0)
    84→		// AND has no LastCheckpointID (not recently condensed)
    85→		// Sessions with LastCheckpointID are valid - they were condensed and the shadow
    86→		// branch was intentionally deleted. Keep them for LastCheckpointID reuse.
    87→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    88→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    89→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    90→			// Shadow branch doesn't exist
    91→			// Only cleanup if session has checkpoints AND no LastCheckpointID
    92→			// Sessions with LastCheckpointID should be kept for checkpoint reuse
    93→			if state.CheckpointCount > 0 && state.LastCheckpointID == "" {
    94→				// Clear the orphaned session state (best-effort, don't fail listing)
    95→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    96→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    97→				continue
    98→			}
    99→			// Keep sessions with LastCheckpointID or no checkpoints yet
   100→		}
   101→
   102→		states = append(states, state)
   103→	}
   104→	return states, nil
   105→}
   106→
   107→// findSessionsForWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   108→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   109→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return nil, err
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	var matching []*SessionState
   115→	for _, state := range allStates {
   116→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   117→			matching = append(matching, state)
   118→		}
   119→	}
   120→	return matching, nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→// findSessionsForCommit finds all sessions where base_commit matches the given SHA.
   124→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   125→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		return nil, err
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	var matching []*SessionState
   131→	for _, state := range allStates {
   132→		if state.BaseCommit == baseCommitSHA {
   133→			matching = append(matching, state)
   134→		}
   135→	}
   136→	return matching, nil
   137→}
   138→
   139→// FindSessionsForCommit is the exported version of findSessionsForCommit.
   140→// Used by the rewind reset command to find sessions to clean up.
   141→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) FindSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   142→	return s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA)
   143→}
   144→
   145→// ClearSessionState is the exported version of clearSessionState.
   146→// Used by the rewind reset command to clean up session state files.
   147→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   148→	return s.clearSessionState(sessionID)
   149→}
   150→
   151→// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints counts how many other active sessions
   152→// from the SAME worktree (different from currentSessionID) have created checkpoints
   153→// on the SAME base commit (current HEAD). This is used to show an informational message
   154→// about concurrent sessions that will be included in the next commit.
   155→// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   156→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error) {
   157→	currentWorktree, err := GetWorktreePath()
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return 0, err
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Get current HEAD to compare with session base commits
   163→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return 0, err
   166→	}
   167→	head, err := repo.Head()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   172→
   173→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		return 0, err
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	count := 0
   179→	for _, state := range allStates {
   180→		// Only consider sessions from the same worktree with checkpoints
   181→		// AND based on the same commit (current HEAD)
   182→		// Sessions from different base commits are independent and shouldn't be counted
   183→		if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
   184→			state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
   185→			state.CheckpointCount > 0 &&
   186→			state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
   187→			count++
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→	return count, nil
   191→}
   192→
   193→// initializeSession creates a new session state or updates a partial one.
   194→// A partial state may exist if the concurrent session warning was shown.
   195→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   196→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   197→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string) (*SessionState, error) {
   198→	head, err := repo.Head()
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Get worktree ID for shadow branch naming
   209→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
   210→	if err != nil {
   211→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Capture untracked files at session start to preserve them during rewind
   215→	untrackedFiles, err := collectUntrackedFiles()
   216→	if err != nil {
   217→		// Non-fatal: continue even if we can't collect untracked files
   218→		untrackedFiles = nil
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	state := &SessionState{
   222→		SessionID:             sessionID,
   223→		BaseCommit:            head.Hash().String(),
   224→		WorktreePath:          worktreePath,
   225→		WorktreeID:            worktreeID,
   226→		StartedAt:             time.Now(),
   227→		CheckpointCount:       0,
   228→		UntrackedFilesAtStart: untrackedFiles,
   229→		AgentType:             agentType,
   230→		TranscriptPath:        transcriptPath,
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   234→		return nil, err
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	return state, nil
   238→}
   239→
   240→// getShadowBranchNameForCommit returns the shadow branch name for the given base commit and worktree ID.
   241→// worktreeID should be empty for the main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   242→func getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID string) string {
   243→	return checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   244→}
   245→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/.claude/plans/prancy-noodling-ember.md",
  "content": "# ENT-221 + ENT-194: Session Phase State Machine\n\n## Context\n\nWhen an AI agent commits via Bash mid-session (between UserPromptSubmit and Stop hooks), `post-commit` fires and prematurely condenses session data, resets checkpoint count, and deletes the shadow branch. This causes:\n\n- **ENT-194**: Sequential agent commits produce empty/incomplete checkpoints because each `post-commit` condenses before the transcript is complete\n- **ENT-221**: No cross-hook state tracking means hooks can't distinguish \"agent committed mid-session\" from \"user committed between sessions\"\n\nThe fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.\n\n## State Machine\n\n```\nUNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──> ACTIVE\nACTIVE ──Stop──────────────────────> IDLE\nACTIVE ──git commit────────────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED\nACTIVE_COMMITTED ──git commit──────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED\nACTIVE_COMMITTED ──Stop────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE (immediate)\nIDLE ──git commit──────────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE\nIDLE ──UserPromptSubmit────────────> ACTIVE\nIDLE ──SessionStop─────────────────> ENDED\nENDED ──git commit─────────────────> ENDED + CONDENSE (remaining data)\nENDED ──resume (UserPromptSubmit)──> ACTIVE (clear EndedAt)\nTIMED_OUT (virtual): LastInteractionTime > 30min, treated as ENDED\n```\n\nKey behaviors:\n- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch\n- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)\n- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed\n- **All sessions included in condensation**: Even sessions with no file changes get transcript captured\n- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### Step 1: File Locking on StateStore\n\nAdd `flock`-based `LoadModifySave()` to `session.StateStore` for atomic read-modify-write.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(*State) error) error`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Concurrent access tests, timeout tests, error propagation tests\n\n**Design:**\n```go\nfunc (s *StateStore) LoadModifySave(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, fn func(*State) error) error {\n    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + \".lock\"\n    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)\n    defer f.Close()\n    defer os.Remove(lockPath)\n    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired\n    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)\n\n    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)\n    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }\n    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }\n    return s.Save(ctx, state)\n}\n```\n\n- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only > AGENT

The plan covers all the decisions from our brainstorm including:

- Phase state machine with ACTIVE, ACTIVE_COMMITTED, IDLE, ENDED
- ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE triggers immediate condensation at Stop
- flock-based file locking for concurrent safety (subagents, multi-terminal)
- PendingCheckpointID shared across all commits in a session
- Shadow branch preserved while any session is ACTIVE
- current-session.txt removal
- Backward compatibility (empty phase = IDLE)

Regarding your question about subagents: they operate within the ACTIVE phase and write to the same shadow branch via `SaveTaskCheckpoint()`. The locking (Step 6) is critical for them since multiple subagents can call `SaveTaskCheckpoint()` concurrently, racing on the same state file. No separate subagent phases are needed — they're just concurrent writers within an ACTIVE session.

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me create a detailed analysis document:

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, I can now provide you with comprehensive answers to your questions:

## Summary of Findings

### 1. **`entire reset` Command**

**What it does:**
- Deletes the shadow branch (`entire/<HEAD-hash>-<worktreeHash>`) for the current HEAD commit
- Clears all session state files (`.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json`) that have `base_commit` matching current HEAD
- Allows starting fresh without existing checkpoints on the current commit

**How it works:**
```go
// From manual_commit_reset.go:
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Reset() error {
    // 1. Get current HEAD and worktree ID
    // 2. Find shadow branch name: entire/<HEAD-hash[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
    // 3. Find all sessions where base_commit == HEAD
    // 4. Delete each session state file
    // 5. Delete the shadow branch if it exists
}
```

**Key behavior:**
- Only works with manual-commit strategy
- Requires confirmation unless `--force` is used
- Reports which session states were cleared and if shadow branch was deleted

---

### 2. **`entire rewind` Command**

**What it does:**
- **For uncommitted checkpoints:** Restores working directory files from a shadow branch checkpoint
- **For committed checkpoints (logs-only):** Offers 3 options:
  1. Restore logs only (keep current files)
  2. Checkout commit (detached HEAD, for viewing)
  3. Reset branch to commit (destructive, uses `git reset --hard`)

**How it works:**
```go
// From manual_commit_rewind.go:
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
    // 1. Get checkpoint commit from shadow branch
    // 2. Reset shadow branch HEAD to this checkpoint (truncates future checkpoints)
    // 3. Build file lists:
    //    - checkpointFiles: files in checkpoint tree
    //    - trackedFiles: files in HEAD
    //    - preservedUntrackedFiles: files that existed at session start
    // 4. Walk working directory and DELETE untracked files that:
    //    - Are NOT in checkpoint
    //    - Are NOT tracked in HEAD
    //    - Did NOT exist at session start
    // 5. Restore all files from checkpoint tree (except .entire/ metadata)
}
```

**Key safety feature:** When rewinding, the shadow branch is reset to the checkpoint:
```go
// resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint ensures next checkpoint only includes prompts from this point forward
shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commit.Hash)
repo.Storer.SetReference(ref)
```

---

### 3. **Shadow Branch Management**

#### **Session State Structure:**
```go
type SessionState struct {
    SessionID        string
    BaseCommit       string  // HEAD commit when session started
    WorktreeID       string  // Empty for main worktree, git worktree name for linked worktrees
    CheckpointCount  int
    LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID  // Reused for commits without new content
    // ... other fields
}
```

#### **Shadow Branch Naming:**
```go
// Format: entire/<HEAD-hash[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
// Example: entire/a3b2c4d-5e6f7a
shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
```

---

### 4. **What Happens During User Git Operations**

#### **Scenario A: User does `git reset --hard`** (HEAD moves backward)

**Current behavior:**
1. HEAD changes to an older commit
2. **Migration detection:** `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` is called at next prompt
3. **Detection logic:**
   ```go
   currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   if state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
       return false, nil // No migration needed
   }
   // HEAD changed - check if old shadow branch exists
   oldShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   newShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(currentHead, state.WorktreeID)
   ```

4. **Migration action:**
   - Renames shadow branch: `entire/<old-hash>-<worktreeID>` → `entire/<new-hash>-<worktreeID>`
   - Updates `state.BaseCommit` to new HEAD
   - Preserves all checkpoints

**Problem identified:** Migration assumes HEAD moved forward (pull/rebase scenario). For `git reset --hard`:
- If resetting to a commit that already has a shadow branch, **collision** occurs
- If old shadow branch has checkpoints from a different session, they're **orphaned**

#### **Scenario B: User does `git stash && git pull && git stash pop`**

**Current behavior:**
1. Stash: Working directory is cleaned (but session state remains)
2. Pull: HEAD moves forward (new commits from remote)
3. **Migration detection:** Detects HEAD change, renames shadow branch
4. Stash pop: Files restored

**Result:** Works correctly - shadow branch follows HEAD

---

### 5. **Migration Logic Analysis**

```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
    currentHead := head.Hash().String()
    if state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
        return false, nil // No migration needed
    }

    // HEAD changed - migrate shadow branch
    oldShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    newShadowBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(currentHead, state.WorktreeID)

    // ISSUE: Guard against hash prefix collision
    if oldShadowBranch == newShadowBranch {
        state.BaseCommit = currentHead
        return true, nil
    }

    oldRef, err := repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
    if err != nil {
        // Old shadow branch doesn't exist - just update BaseCommit
        state.BaseCommit = currentHead
        return true, nil
    }

    // Old shadow branch exists - move it to new base commit
    // ISSUE: What if newShadowBranch already exists from a different session?
    newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(newRefName, oldRef.Hash())
    repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef)  // Overwrites if exists!

    repo.Storer.RemoveReference(oldRefName)
    state.BaseCommit = currentHead
    return true, nil
}
```

**Key insight:** Migration does NOT check if the target shadow branch already exists. This means:
- Resetting to an older commit with existing shadow branch = **overwrites** that branch
- Original checkpoints from the older session are **lost**

---

### 6. **Condensation and Cleanup Flow**

#### **prepare-commit-msg hook:**
```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile, source string) error {
    // 1. Find active sessions for current worktree
    sessions := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
    
    // 2. Filter sessions with new content (transcript grew since last condensation)
    sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
    
    // 3. If new content: generate new checkpoint ID
    // 4. If no new content: reuse LastCheckpointID from session (if exists)
    // 5. Add Entire-Checkpoint trailer to commit message
}
```

#### **post-commit hook:**
```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
    // 1. Check if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
    checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
    if !found {
        return nil // User removed trailer - no condensation
    }
    
    // 2. Find sessions with new content
    // 3. Condense each session to entire/sessions branch
    // 4. Update session state:
    state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()  // NEW HEAD!
    state.CheckpointCount = 0                // Reset count
    state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID    // Save for reuse
    
    // 5. Delete shadow branch (data now on entire/sessions)
    deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName)
}
```

**Critical insight:** After condensation:
- `BaseCommit` is updated to the NEW commit (just created)
- `CheckpointCount` is reset to 0
- Shadow branch is **deleted**
- Session state file is **kept** with `LastCheckpointID` for potential reuse

---

## Issues and Edge Cases

### **Issue 1: Reset to older commit loses checkpoints**

**Scenario:**
1. Session A at commit `abc1234` creates checkpoints → shadow branch `entire/abc1234-<worktree>`
2. User creates commit `def5678`
3. Migration moves shadow branch → `entire/def5678-<worktree>`
4. User does `git reset --hard abc1234` (back to original commit)
5. Migration tries to move shadow branch → `entire/abc1234-<worktree>` (collision with session A!)
6. **Result:** Session A's checkpoints are overwritten

### **Issue 2: Concurrent sessions and reset**

**Scenario:**
1. Session A running at commit `abc1234`
2. User creates commit `def5678` (BaseCommit updated via PostCommit)
3. Session B starts at commit `def5678` (concurrent with session A)
4. User does `git reset --hard abc1234`
5. **Result:** Both sessions try to use `entire/abc1234-<worktree>` shadow branch

### **Issue 3: Orphaned branch cleanup timing**

From `listAllSessionStates()`:
```go
// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists
// Only cleanup if: CheckpointCount > 0 AND LastCheckpointID == ""
if state.CheckpointCount > 0 && state.LastCheckpointID == "" {
    store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    continue
}
```

**Issue:** Cleanup happens during listing, which can surprise users. If they do:
1. `git reset --hard` (BaseCommit now stale)
2. `entire rewind --list` (triggers cleanup, deletes session state)
3. Session work is lost without warning

---

## Recommendations

### **1. Reset detection and warning:**
- Detect when HEAD moves backward (not just forward)
- Check if target commit already has a shadow branch
- Prompt user about potential checkpoint loss

### **2. Shadow branch collision handling:**
- Before migration, check if target shadow branch exists
- If exists, either:
  - Refuse migration and warn user
  - Merge checkpoints from both sessions
  - Create a backup branch

### **3. Safer cleanup:**
- Don't auto-cleanup during `listAllSessionStates()`
- Require explicit `entire reset` command
- Show preview of what will be deleted

### **4. Session state tracking:**
- Add `OriginalBaseCommit` field to track where session truly started
- Detect "reset loops" (repeatedly resetting between same commits)

This analysis reveals that while the system handles forward HEAD movement (pull/rebase) well, it has gaps around backward movement (reset/revert) and shadow branch collision scenarios.

### `entire reset` and Session Phase

`entire reset` does:
1. Finds shadow branch for current HEAD (`entire/<HEAD[:7]>-<worktreeID[:6]>`)
2. Finds all session states where `BaseCommit == HEAD`
3. Deletes each session state file
4. Deletes the shadow branch

**Impact on our model:** `entire reset` nukes everything — state files, shadow branch, all phases. This is fine for IDLE/ENDED sessions. But if called while a session is ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED:
- The agent is still running, will try to SaveChanges later
- SaveChanges will fail to find the session state → it falls back to `initializeSession()` (line 176 of `manual_commit_git.go`)
- So it self-heals, but loses all previous checkpoint data

We should add a **guard**: if any session on the shadow branch has phase ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED, warn/refuse unless `--force`.

### `git reset --hard` or `git stash` while IDLE

Neither operation fires any git hooks (no `prepare-commit-msg`, `post-commit`). So the session phase stays IDLE and nothing happens until the next UserPromptSubmit.

**`git stash` (no HEAD change):**
- Working directory cleaned, but HEAD unchanged
- Shadow branch + session state untouched
- `git stash pop` restores files
- Next UserPromptSubmit: no migration needed (BaseCommit matches)
- **Safe.** Data preserved.

**`git reset --hard` (to HEAD, no target):**
- Cleans working directory to HEAD state
- HEAD unchanged → no migration
- Agent's uncommitted changes gone from working directory, but **still on shadow branch**
- User can `entire rewind` to restore
- **Safe.** Data preserved on shadow branch.

**`git reset --hard <older-commit>` (HEAD moves backward):**
- HEAD moves to an older commit
- No hooks fire (no post-commit)
- Session state still says IDLE with old BaseCommit
- On next `UserPromptSubmit` → `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` detects HEAD change
- **Migration renames shadow branch**: `entire/<old[:7]>-<wt>` → `entire/<new[:7]>-<wt>`
- **Collision risk**: if the older commit already had a shadow branch, it gets overwritten silently

**`git reset --hard <older-commit>` while ACTIVE_COMMITTED:**
- Same as above but worse — the agent is still running
- When agent does another commit, post-commit fires with wrong BaseCommit
- Migration hasn't happened yet (that's done on UserPromptSubmit, not post-commit)
- **Currently post-commit doesn't migrate** — it just reads BaseCommit from state

This is the gap: post-commit uses `state.BaseCommit` to find the shadow branch, but HEAD has moved. The shadow branch is still at the old name. Post-commit would fail to find sessions or find the wrong shadow branch.

However, with our new model where post-commit **skips condensation** for ACTIVE sessions and only updates BaseCommit + migrates, this is actually handled. The migration in post-commit (Step 4) would rename the shadow branch.

Let me update the plan with these edge cases.

     1→# ENT-221 + ENT-194: Session Phase State Machine
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→When an AI agent commits via Bash mid-session (between UserPromptSubmit and Stop hooks), `post-commit` fires and prematurely condenses session data, resets checkpoint count, and deletes the shadow branch. This causes:
     6→
     7→- **ENT-194**: Sequential agent commits produce empty/incomplete checkpoints because each `post-commit` condenses before the transcript is complete
     8→- **ENT-221**: No cross-hook state tracking means hooks can't distinguish "agent committed mid-session" from "user committed between sessions"
     9→
    10→The fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.
    11→
    12→## State Machine
    13→
    14→```
    15→UNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──> ACTIVE
    16→ACTIVE ──Stop──────────────────────> IDLE
    17→ACTIVE ──git commit────────────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    18→ACTIVE_COMMITTED ──git commit──────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    19→ACTIVE_COMMITTED ──Stop────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE (immediate)
    20→IDLE ──git commit──────────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE
    21→IDLE ──UserPromptSubmit────────────> ACTIVE
    22→IDLE ──SessionStop─────────────────> ENDED
    23→ENDED ──git commit─────────────────> ENDED + CONDENSE (remaining data)
    24→ENDED ──resume (UserPromptSubmit)──> ACTIVE (clear EndedAt)
    25→TIMED_OUT (virtual): LastInteractionTime > 30min, treated as ENDED
    26→```
    27→
    28→Key behaviors:
    29→- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch
    30→- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)
    31→- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed
    32→- **All sessions included in condensation**: Even sessions with no file changes get transcript captured
    33→- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session
    34→
    35→## Implementation Steps
    36→
    37→### Step 1: File Locking on StateStore
    38→
    39→Add `flock`-based `LoadModifySave()` to `session.StateStore` for atomic read-modify-write.
    40→
    41→**Files:**
    42→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(*State) error) error`
    43→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Concurrent access tests, timeout tests, error propagation tests
    44→
    45→**Design:**
    46→```go
    47→func (s *StateStore) LoadModifySave(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, fn func(*State) error) error {
    48→    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + ".lock"
    49→    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
    50→    defer f.Close()
    51→    defer os.Remove(lockPath)
    52→    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired
    53→    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)
    54→
    55→    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
    56→    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }
    57→    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }
    58→    return s.Save(ctx, state)
    59→}
    60→```
    61→
    62→- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only operations (listing, status)
    63→- `LoadModifySave` is the only way to mutate state safely
    64→

The plan now covers both edge cases. To summarize what I found:

- **`entire reset`** — currently nukes everything. We should add a guard that refuses to reset while any session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED (unless `--force`).
- **`git stash` while IDLE** — safe, no hooks fire, no HEAD change.
- **`git reset --hard` while IDLE** — working directory changes but shadow branch preserves data. Next `UserPromptSubmit` handles migration if HEAD changed.
- **`git reset --hard <older>` while ACTIVE_COMMITTED** — the riskiest case. Our new post-commit (Step 4) handles this because it calls `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` as part of the "update BaseCommit" logic for active sessions.
- **Shadow branch collision on backward reset** — pre-existing issue, out of scope for this PR.

tracking means hooks can't distinguish \"agent committed mid-session\" from \"user committed between sessions\"\n\nThe fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.\n\n## State Machine\n\n```\nUNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──> ACTIVE\nACTIVE **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch\n- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits string, fn func(*State) error) error {\n    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + \".lock\"\n    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)\n    defer f.Close()\n    defer os.Remove(lockPath)\n    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired\n    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)\n\n    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)\n    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }\n    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }\n    return s.Save(ctx, state)\n}\n```\n\n- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only > AGENT

Correct. Claude Code has a `SessionEnd` hook, but on Ctrl-C (SIGINT) or terminal close, there's no guarantee it fires. The session would be stuck in ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED until the TTL expires.

**Impact of being stuck in ACTIVE:**
- User commits → post-commit sees ACTIVE → skips condensation → user's commits have no metadata linked
- This continues until LastInteractionTime exceeds TTL

**The 30-minute TTL is too long for this.** An agent that's genuinely working interacts every few seconds (tool calls, saves). If it's been 2+ minutes with no interaction, the session is almost certainly dead.

**Proposed refinement:** Use a **tiered TTL**:
- **Post-commit context** (git hooks): 2-minute TTL. If LastInteractionTime > 2 min and phase is ACTIVE, treat as TIMED_OUT. This gives fast recovery after Ctrl-C while avoiding false positives (agents are chatty).
- **UserPromptSubmit context** (new session starting): 30-minute TTL for cleanup of other sessions. More conservative since it's just housekeeping.

Additionally, we could add a **cleanup sweep on UserPromptSubmit**: when any session starts, scan other sessions in the same worktree and clean up stale ACTIVE phases. This provides natural garbage collection.

essentially it's back to IDLE. I don't think there's a hook we can tap into for this (there may be an open Issue on claude code)

> AGENT

Good distinction. Two separate scenarios with different characteristics:

**Scenario 1: Ctrl-C interruption (agent survives)**
- Agent is back at prompt, session alive, but no hook fires
- Session stuck in ACTIVE even though agent is idle at the prompt
- If user types another prompt → UserPromptSubmit fires → transition works fine
- If user commits immediately → post-commit sees ACTIVE → skips condensation (wrong)
- **No known hook for this.** The data is safe on the shadow branch — it just won't condense until either the next Stop or the session truly ends

**Scenario 2: Process killed (agent dies)**
- No hooks fire at all
- TTL-based recovery kicks in
- But: agent might be alive but blocked on permission prompt → looks inactive but isn't
- Or: subagent/tool use hooks push out LastInteractionTime → TTL stays fresh even if main agent is idle

For scenario 2, the permission prompt case is interesting — the agent is alive and waiting for user input. If we set a 2-min TTL, a user who walks away from a permission prompt for 3 minutes then comes back and commits would get false-positive TIMED_OUT. But that's probably fine — condensation would happen, and the data is correct (the session was effectively idle during that time).

For scenario 1, I think the honest answer is: **this is a gap we accept for now** and track as a known limitation. The data is preserved (shadow branch), it just won't condense on that immediate commit. It'll condense on the next commit after Stop fires or the session ends.

Let me update the plan.

> AGENT

Let me think through this systematically — checking each event against each phase for gaps:

**Events we haven't discussed:**

1. **`git rebase` during ACTIVE session** — `prepare-commit-msg` already skips during sequence operations (rebase, cherry-pick, revert). `post-commit` fires for each rebase-created commit though. With our changes, post-commit sees ACTIVE → transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrates shadow branch per commit. After rebase completes, shadow branch is at the new HEAD. Seems fine, but many rapid shadow branch migrations could be noisy.

2. **`git rebase --abort` after partial rebase** — HEAD resets to pre-rebase state. No hooks fire on abort. Session state has updated BaseCommit from the partial rebase commits. On next UserPromptSubmit, migration detects HEAD change and renames shadow branch back. Works.

3. **False-positive TIMED_OUT on permission prompts** — Agent is alive but waiting for user to approve a tool use. LastInteractionTime is stale (> 2 min). User commits in another terminal → post-commit falsely treats as TIMED_OUT → condenses prematurely. Agent continues → SaveChanges creates new shadow branch, data accumulates fresh. Result: session data is split across two condensations. Not ideal but not data loss.

4. **User removes trailer from commit while session is ACTIVE** — prepare-commit-msg adds it, user deletes it in editor. post-commit checks for trailer, doesn't find one, exits without doing anything. Session stays ACTIVE. PendingCheckpointID preserved for next commit. Works correctly.

5. **SaveChanges with no file changes** — Agent ran but only chatted, no file modifications. Shadow branch commit still created with metadata. Condensation still captures transcript. This matches the design intent ("include all sessions even without file changes").

6. **Auto-commit strategy** — Phase field exists on `session.State` which both strategies share, but auto-commit ignores it. Our changes only modify manual-commit code paths. Auto-commit is unaffected.

I think the main notable gap is **#3 (false-positive timeout on permission prompts)**. The impact is a split in session data — not data loss, just two condensation events instead of one. Given that permission prompts rarely exceed 2 minutes, and the fallback is graceful, I think it's acceptable.

Everything else looks covered. Let me finalize the plan.

> AGENT

I believe we've covered all the meaningful edge cases. The plan documents:

- **Normal flow**: ACTIVE → git commit → ACTIVE_COMMITTED → Stop → condense
- **User operations in IDLE**: stash (safe), reset (migration handles it)
- **Concurrent sessions**: independent phases, shared shadow branch protected
- **Rebase during ACTIVE**: handled via existing skip + migration
- **Ctrl-C interruption**: known gap, data safe on shadow branch
- **Permission prompt timeout**: graceful degradation (split condensation)
- **`entire reset` with active session**: guard added
- **Process death**: TTL-based recovery

This causes:\n\n- **ENT-194**: Sequential agent commits produce empty/incomplete checkpoints because each `post-commit` condenses before the transcript is complete\n- **ENT-221**: No cross-hook state tracking means hooks can't distinguish \"agent committed mid-session\" from \"user committed between sessions\"\n\nThe fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.\n\n## State Machine\n\n```\nUNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──> ACTIVE\nACTIVE ──Stop──────────────────────> IDLE\nACTIVE ──git commit────────────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED\nACTIVE_COMMITTED ──git commit──────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED\nACTIVE_COMMITTED ──Stop────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE (immediate)\nIDLE ──git commit──────────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE\nIDLE ──UserPromptSubmit────────────> ACTIVE\nIDLE ──SessionStop─────────────────> ENDED\nENDED ──git commit─────────────────> ENDED + CONDENSE (remaining data)\nENDED ──resume (UserPromptSubmit)──> ACTIVE (clear EndedAt)\nTIMED_OUT (virtual): LastInteractionTime > TTL, treated as ENDED\n```\n\n### Timeout Detection\n\n**Tiered TTL:** Different contexts use different staleness thresholds:\n- **Post-commit hook**: 2-minute TTL. Agents interact frequently (tool calls every few seconds), so 2 min of silence strongly indicates the session is dead or interrupted. Fast recovery for Ctrl-C/kill scenarios.\n- **General cleanup** (UserPromptSubmit scanning other sessions): 30-minute TTL. More conservative since it's housekeeping.\n\n### Known Limitation: False-Positive Timeout on Permission Prompts\n\nIf the agent is alive but blocked on a permission prompt for > 2 minutes and the user commits in another terminal, post-commit falsely treats the session as TIMED_OUT. Impact: premature condensation + shadow branch deletion. When the agent continues, SaveChanges creates a new shadow branch — session data is split across two condensations. Not data loss, just two entries instead of one. The session stays in ACTIVE even though the agent is idle.\n\n- If user types another prompt → UserPromptSubmit fires → handles transition correctly\n- If user commits immediately → post-commit sees ACTIVE with recent LastInteractionTime → skips condensation\n- **Data is safe** (shadow branch preserves it), just won't condense on that commit\n- Will condense on the next commit after Stop fires, session ends, or TTL expires\n- Potential future fix: Claude Code adds an \"interrupted\" hook (track upstream)\n\nKey behaviors:\n- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch\n- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)\n- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed\n- **All sessions included in condensation**: Even sessions with no file changes get transcript captured\n- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### Step 1: File Locking on StateStore\n\nAdd `flock`-based `LoadModifySave()` to `session.StateStore` for atomic read-modify-write.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(*State) error) error`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Concurrent access tests, timeout tests, error propagation tests\n\n**Design:**\n```go\nfunc (s *StateStore) LoadModifySave(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, fn func(*State) error) error {\n    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + \".lock\"\n    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)\n    defer f.Close()\n    defer os.Remove(lockPath)\n    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired\n    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)\n\n    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)\n    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }\n    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }\n    return s.Save(ctx, state)\n}\n```\n\n- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only operations (listing, status)\n- `LoadModifySave` is the only way to mutate state safely\n\n### Step 2: Phase Field on Session State\n\nAdd `Phase` enum and `PendingCheckpointID` to both `session.State` and `strategy.SessionState`.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `Phase` type + constants, `PendingCheckpointID` field, helper methods (`IsActive()`, `CanCondense()`, `IsTimedOut()`)\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` — Mirror `Phase` and `PendingCheckpointID` fields on `SessionState`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit.go` — Update `sessionStateToStrategy()` and `sessionStateFromStrategy()` conversion functions\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Phase transition tests, backward compat (missing phase = IDLE)\n\n**New types in `session/state.go`:**\n```go\ntype Phase string\nconst (\n    PhaseActive          Phase = \"active\"\n    PhaseActiveCommitted Phase = \"active_committed\"\n    PhaseIdle            Phase = \"idle\"\n    PhaseEnded           Phase = \"ended\"\n)\n```\n\n**New fields on `State`:**\n```go\nPhase               Phase           `json:\"phase,omitempty\"`             // empty = idle (backward compat)\nPendingCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:\"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty\"`\n```\n\n**Backward compat:** Missing/empty Phase treated as `PhaseIdle`.\n\n### Step 3: Phase Transitions in Agent Hooks\n\nWire up `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, and `SessionStop` to set phases using `LoadModifySave`.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `InitializeSession()`: set Phase=ACTIVE, clear EndedAt on resume, update LastInteractionTime\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`: use `LoadModifySave`, update LastInteractionTime. Check if Phase==ACTIVE_COMMITTED and trigger condensation on Stop transition.\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `commitWithMetadata()` (Stop handler): after SaveChanges, check if phase was ACTIVE_COMMITTED and condense if so. Transition phase to IDLE.\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `markSessionEnded()`: use `LoadModifySave`, set Phase=ENDED\n\n**InitializeSession changes:**\n- IDLE/ENDED → ACTIVE: Set phase, clear EndedAt, generate PendingCheckpointID if missing\n- ACTIVE → ACTIVE: (same session, new prompt) Just update attribution + LastInteractionTime\n\n**Stop handler changes (commitWithMetadata):**\n- Load state, check phase BEFORE calling SaveChanges\n- After SaveChanges:\n  - If was ACTIVE_COMMITTED → condense immediately, set phase=IDLE\n  - If was ACTIVE → set phase=IDLE (normal, condense on user commit)\n\n### Step 4: Phase-Aware PostCommit\n\nUpdate `PostCommit()` to check phase and defer condensation for active sessions.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PostCommit()`: for ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions, skip condensation, update BaseCommit + migrate shadow branch, set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_migration.go` — Ensure `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded` works when called from PostCommit context\n\n**PostCommit logic per session:**\n```\nif phase == ACTIVE:\n    phase = ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n    migrate shadow branch to new HEAD\n    update BaseCommit\n    SKIP condensation\n\nif phase == ACTIVE_COMMITTED:\n    migrate shadow branch to new HEAD\n    update BaseCommit\n    SKIP condensation\n\nif phase == IDLE or ENDED or TIMED_OUT:\n    condense (existing behavior)\n```\n\n**Shadow branch deletion:**\n```\ncanDelete = true\nfor each session on this shadow branch:\n    if session.Phase == ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED:\n        canDelete = false\nif canDelete:\n    delete shadow branch\n```\n\n### Step 5: Phase-Aware PrepareCommitMsg\n\nUpdate `PrepareCommitMsg()` to use `PendingCheckpointID` from session state.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PrepareCommitMsg()`: use existing PendingCheckpointID from state instead of generating new one each time\n\n**Logic:**\n- If any session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID: use it\n- If no PendingCheckpointID exists: generate one, store in state via LoadModifySave\n- All commits in a session share the same PendingCheckpointID\n\n### Step 6: Migrate Locking to All Call Sites\n\nReplace bare load+save patterns with `LoadModifySave` across all mutation sites.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`, `SaveTaskCheckpoint()`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PostCommit()`, `InitializeSession()`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — Add `loadModifySaveSessionState()` wrapper\n\n**Approach:** The strategy layer calls `stateStore.LoadModifySave()` via a helper that handles the `session.State` ↔ `strategy.SessionState` conversion:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadModifySaveSessionState(\n    sessionID string, fn func(*SessionState) error,\n) error {\n    store, _ := s.getStateStore()\n    return store.LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(state *session.State) error {\n        stratState := sessionStateToStrategy(state)\n        if err := fn(stratState); err != nil { return err }\n        *state = *sessionStateFromStrategy(stratState)\n        return nil\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### Step 7: Remove current-session.txt\n\nRemove legacy single-session tracking.\n\n**Files to modify:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go:282` — Remove `paths.WriteCurrentSession()` call\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_geminicli_handlers.go:48` — Remove `paths.ReadCurrentSession()` usage (get session ID from hook input instead)\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_git_cmd.go:67` — `initHookLogging()`: get session ID from session state files instead of `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hook_logging_test.go` — Update tests\n\n### Step 8: Integration Tests\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/phase_state_test.go` — New file\n\n**Test scenarios:**\n1. Mid-session commit: UserPromptSubmit → SaveChanges → git commit → verify ACTIVE_COMMITTED, no condensation\n2. Stop after mid-session commit: verify condensation happens at Stop\n3. Multiple mid-session commits: verify single condensation at Stop\n4. Concurrent sessions: one commits mid-session, verify independent phases\n5. Session resume: ENDED → ACTIVE clears EndedAt\n6. Shadow branch preserved: verify shadow branch NOT deleted while ACTIVE session exists\n7. `entire reset` with ACTIVE session: verify warning/refusal\n8. `git reset --hard` during ACTIVE_COMMITTED: verify post-commit migrates correctly\n\n### Step 9: CLAUDE.md Updates\n\nUpdate documentation to reflect:\n- Phase state machine description\n- New session state fields\n- Updated multi-session behavior\n- Locking behavior\n\n## Edge Cases: External Git Operations\n\n### `entire reset` while session is active\n\n`entire reset` deletes the shadow branch and all session state files for the current HEAD. If called while a session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED:\n- Agent's subsequent SaveChanges will fail to find state, falls back to `initializeSession()` (self-heals)\n- But all previous checkpoint data is lost\n\n**Action (Step 4):** Add guard in `Reset()` — if any session on the shadow branch has phase ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED, warn and require `--force`.\n\n### `git stash` while IDLE\n\n- HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire\n- Shadow branch + session state untouched\n- `git stash pop` restores files\n- **Safe.** No changes needed.\n\n### `git reset --hard` (to HEAD) while IDLE\n\n- Cleans working directory, HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire\n- Agent's changes gone from working directory but **preserved on shadow branch**\n- User can `entire rewind` to restore\n- **Safe.** No changes needed.\n\n### `git reset --hard <older-commit>` while IDLE\n\n- HEAD moves backward, no hooks fire\n- Session state still has old BaseCommit\n- On next `UserPromptSubmit` → `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` renames shadow branch\n- **Collision risk:** if the older commit already has a shadow branch, it gets overwritten\n- This is a pre-existing issue, not introduced by our changes\n- **Out of scope** for this PR, but noted for future hardening\n\n### `git rebase` while ACTIVE\n\n`prepare-commit-msg` already skips during sequence operations (rebase, cherry-pick, revert) — no trailer added. `post-commit` fires for each rebased commit: sees ACTIVE → transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrates shadow branch. After rebase, shadow branch is at the new HEAD. `git rebase --abort` resets HEAD; next UserPromptSubmit migrates back. **Handled**, but many rapid migrations could be noisy (logging only, not user-visible).\n\n### `git reset --hard` while ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\n- HEAD moves but agent is still running\n- Agent may do another commit → post-commit fires\n- Our new post-commit (Step 4) calls `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` which handles the HEAD change\n- **Handled** by the migration in post-commit.\n\n## Coordination\n\n- **PR #165 (LastInteractionTime)**: This plan uses LastInteractionTime for crash/timeout detection. If PR #165 hasn't landed, we add the field ourselves. Start a session, have agent commit mid-session, verify no condensation\n2. Stop the session, verify condensation happens\n3. Commit normally, verify trailer links to correct checkpoint\n4. Check `entire status` shows correct phase info\n5. Kill a session mid-work, verify timeout detection works on next commit\n"

> AGENT

Yes — that's a useful recovery signal. If `UserPromptSubmit` fires and the session is already ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED, we know the previous turn ended without a proper Stop. That means:

1. **ACTIVE → UserPromptSubmit**: Previous turn was interrupted (Ctrl-C) or Stop hook failed. The live transcript has uncaptured content from that turn.
2. **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → UserPromptSubmit**: Same, but the agent also committed during the interrupted turn. Shadow branch may have partial data but the final portion is uncaptured.

**Recovery action:** Before transitioning to ACTIVE for the new turn, we can do a **catch-up checkpoint** — read the live transcript from the last known position, create a shadow branch checkpoint for the interrupted turn's data. This ensures the interrupted turn's transcript is preserved.

```
UserPromptSubmit:
  if phase == ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED:
    // Previous turn didn't complete properly
    // Do catch-up: read transcript from CondensedTranscriptLines,
    // create shadow branch checkpoint with whatever content exists
    catchUpCheckpoint(state)
  
  // Now start the new turn
  phase = ACTIVE
  calculate attribution
  ...
```

This doesn't fix the "user commits immediately after Ctrl-C" case (no UserPromptSubmit fires between interrupt and commit), but it ensures that **if the user continues using the agent**, the interrupted turn's data is captured before the new turn begins.

err != nil { return err }\n    return s.Save(ctx, state)\n}\n```\n\n- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only operations (listing, status)\n- `LoadModifySave` is the only way to mutate state safely\n\n### Step 2: Phase Field on Session State\n\nAdd `Phase` enum and `PendingCheckpointID` to both `session.State` and `strategy.SessionState`.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `Phase` type + constants, `PendingCheckpointID` field, helper methods (`IsActive()`, `CanCondense()`, `IsTimedOut()`)\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` — Mirror `Phase` and `PendingCheckpointID` fields on `SessionState`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit.go` — Update `sessionStateToStrategy()` and `sessionStateFromStrategy()` conversion functions\n- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Phase transition tests, backward compat (missing phase = IDLE)\n\n**New types in `session/state.go`:**\n```go\ntype Phase string\nconst (\n    PhaseActive          Phase = \"active\"\n    PhaseActiveCommitted Phase = \"active_committed\"\n    PhaseIdle            Phase = \"idle\"\n    PhaseEnded           Phase = \"ended\"\n)\n```\n\n**New fields on `State`:**\n```go\nPhase               Phase           `json:\"phase,omitempty\"`             // empty = idle (backward compat)\nPendingCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:\"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty\"`\n```\n\n**Backward compat:** Missing/empty Phase treated as `PhaseIdle`.\n\n### Step 3: Phase Transitions in Agent Hooks\n\nWire up `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, and `SessionStop` to set phases using `LoadModifySave`.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `InitializeSession()`: set Phase=ACTIVE, clear EndedAt on resume, update LastInteractionTime\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`: use `LoadModifySave`, update LastInteractionTime. Check if Phase==ACTIVE_COMMITTED and trigger condensation on Stop transition.\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `commitWithMetadata()` (Stop handler): after SaveChanges, check if phase was ACTIVE_COMMITTED and condense if so. Transition phase to IDLE.\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `markSessionEnded()`: use `LoadModifySave`, set Phase=ENDED\n\n**InitializeSession changes:**\n- IDLE/ENDED → ACTIVE: Set phase, clear EndedAt, generate PendingCheckpointID if missing\n- ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED → ACTIVE: Previous turn was interrupted (Ctrl-C, crash). **Catch-up checkpoint**: read live transcript from `CondensedTranscriptLines`/`TranscriptLinesAtStart`, create shadow branch checkpoint for the interrupted turn's data. Then reset to ACTIVE for new turn. This ensures interrupted turns don't lose transcript data.\n\n**Stop handler changes (commitWithMetadata):**\n- Load state, check phase BEFORE calling SaveChanges\n- After SaveChanges:\n  - If was ACTIVE_COMMITTED → condense immediately, set phase=IDLE\n  - If was ACTIVE → set phase=IDLE (normal, condense on user commit)\n\n### Step 4: Phase-Aware PostCommit\n\nUpdate `PostCommit()` to check phase and defer condensation for active sessions.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PostCommit()`: for ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions, skip condensation, update BaseCommit + migrate shadow branch, set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_migration.go` — Ensure `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded` works when called from PostCommit context\n\n**PostCommit logic per session:**\n```\nif phase == ACTIVE:\n    phase = ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n    migrate shadow branch to new HEAD\n    update BaseCommit\n    SKIP condensation\n\nif phase == ACTIVE_COMMITTED:\n    migrate shadow branch to new HEAD\n    update BaseCommit\n    SKIP condensation\n\nif phase == IDLE or ENDED or TIMED_OUT:\n    condense (existing behavior)\n```\n\n**Shadow branch deletion:**\n```\ncanDelete = true\nfor each session on this shadow branch:\n    if session.Phase == ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED:\n        canDelete = false\nif canDelete:\n    delete shadow branch\n```\n\n### Step 5: Phase-Aware PrepareCommitMsg\n\nUpdate `PrepareCommitMsg()` to use `PendingCheckpointID` from session state.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PrepareCommitMsg()`: use existing PendingCheckpointID from state instead of generating new one each time\n\n**Logic:**\n- If any session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID: use it\n- If no PendingCheckpointID exists: generate one, store in state via LoadModifySave\n- All commits in a session share the same PendingCheckpointID\n\n### Step 6: Migrate Locking to All Call Sites\n\nReplace bare load+save patterns with `LoadModifySave` across all mutation sites.\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`, `SaveTaskCheckpoint()`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PostCommit()`, `InitializeSession()`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — Add `loadModifySaveSessionState()` wrapper\n\n**Approach:** The strategy layer calls `stateStore.LoadModifySave()` via a helper that handles the `session.State` ↔ `strategy.SessionState` conversion:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadModifySaveSessionState(\n    sessionID string, fn func(*SessionState) error,\n) error {\n    store, _ := s.getStateStore()\n    return store.LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(state *session.State) error {\n        stratState := sessionStateToStrategy(state)\n        if err := fn(stratState); err != nil { return err }\n        *state = *sessionStateFromStrategy(stratState)\n        return nil\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### Step 7: Remove current-session.txt\n\nRemove legacy single-session tracking.\n\n**Files to modify:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go:282` — Remove `paths.WriteCurrentSession()` call\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_geminicli_handlers.go:48` — Remove `paths.ReadCurrentSession()` usage (get session ID from hook input instead)\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_git_cmd.go:67` — `initHookLogging()`: get session ID from session state files instead of current_session file\n- `cmd/entire/cli/debug.go:126` — `debugShowCommand`: list active sessions from StateStore instead\n- `cmd/entire/cli/paths/paths.go` — Remove `ReadCurrentSession()`, `WriteCurrentSession()`, `CurrentSessionFile`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/paths/paths_test.go` — Remove related tests\n- `cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry_test.go` — Update tests that reference current_session\n- `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hook_logging_test.go` — Update tests\n\n### Step 8: Integration Tests\n\n**Files:**\n- `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/phase_state_test.go` — New file\n\n**Test scenarios:**\n1. Mid-session commit: UserPromptSubmit → SaveChanges → git commit → verify ACTIVE_COMMITTED, no condensation\n2. Stop after mid-session commit: verify condensation happens at Stop\n3. Multiple mid-session commits: verify single condensation at Stop\n4. Concurrent sessions: one commits mid-session, verify independent phases\n5. Session resume: ENDED → ACTIVE clears EndedAt\n6. Shadow branch preserved: verify shadow branch NOT deleted while ACTIVE session exists\n7. `entire reset` with ACTIVE session: verify warning/refusal\n8. `git reset --hard` during ACTIVE_COMMITTED: verify post-commit migrates correctly\n\n### Step 9: CLAUDE.md Updates\n\nUpdate documentation to reflect:\n- Phase state machine description\n- New session state fields\n- Updated multi-session behavior\n- Locking behavior\n\n## Edge Cases: External Git Operations\n\n### `entire reset` while session is active\n\n`entire reset` deletes the shadow branch and all session state files for the current HEAD. If called while a session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED:\n- Agent's subsequent SaveChanges will fail to find state, falls back to `initializeSession()` (self-heals)\n- But all previous checkpoint data is lost\n\n**Action (Step 4):** Add guard in `Reset()` — if any session on the shadow branch has phase ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED, warn and require `--force`.\n\n### `git stash` while IDLE\n\n- HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire\n- Shadow branch + session state untouched\n- `git stash pop` restores files\n- **Safe.** No changes needed.\n\n### `git reset --hard` (to HEAD) while IDLE\n\n- Cleans working directory, HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire\n- Agent's changes gone from working directory but **preserved on shadow branch**\n- User can `entire rewind` to restore\n- **Safe.** No changes needed.\n\n### `git reset --hard <older-commit>` while IDLE\n\n- HEAD moves backward, no hooks fire\n- Session state still has old BaseCommit\n- On next `UserPromptSubmit` → `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` renames shadow branch\n- **Collision risk:** if the older commit already has a shadow branch, it gets overwritten\n- This is a pre-existing issue, not introduced by our changes\n- **Out of scope** for this PR, but noted for future hardening\n\n### `git rebase` while ACTIVE\n\n`prepare-commit-msg` already skips during sequence operations (rebase, cherry-pick, revert) — no trailer added. `post-commit` fires for each rebased commit: sees ACTIVE → transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrates shadow branch. After rebase, shadow branch is at the new HEAD. `git rebase --abort` resets HEAD; next UserPromptSubmit migrates back. **Handled**, but many rapid migrations could be noisy (logging only, not user-visible).\n\n### `git reset --hard` while ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\n- HEAD moves but agent is still running\n- Agent may do another commit → post-commit fires\n- Our new post-commit (Step 4) calls `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` which handles the HEAD change\n- **Handled** by the migration in post-commit.\n\n## Coordination\n\n- **PR #165 (LastInteractionTime)**: This plan uses LastInteractionTime for crash/timeout detection. If PR #165 hasn't landed, we add the field ourselves. If it has, we reuse it.\n- **entire/sessions → entire/checkpoints/v1 rename**: Account for the branch rename in any new code.\n\n## Verification\n\nAfter implementation:\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n\nManual verification:\n1. Start a session, have agent commit mid-session, verify no condensation\n2. Stop the session, verify condensation happens\n3. Commit normally, verify trailer links to correct checkpoint\n4. Check `entire status` shows correct phase info\n5. Kill a session mid-work, verify timeout detection works on next commit\n"

     1→# ENT-221 + ENT-194: Session Phase State Machine
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→When an AI agent commits via Bash mid-session (between UserPromptSubmit and Stop hooks), `post-commit` fires and prematurely condenses session data, resets checkpoint count, and deletes the shadow branch. This causes:
     6→
     7→- **ENT-194**: Sequential agent commits produce empty/incomplete checkpoints because each `post-commit` condenses before the transcript is complete
     8→- **ENT-221**: No cross-hook state tracking means hooks can't distinguish "agent committed mid-session" from "user committed between sessions"
     9→
    10→The fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.
    11→
    12→## State Machine
    13→
    14→```
    15→UNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──> ACTIVE
    16→ACTIVE ──Stop──────────────────────> IDLE
    17→ACTIVE ──git commit────────────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    18→ACTIVE_COMMITTED ──git commit──────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    19→ACTIVE_COMMITTED ──Stop────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE (immediate)
    20→IDLE ──git commit──────────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE
    21→IDLE ──UserPromptSubmit────────────> ACTIVE
    22→IDLE ──SessionStop─────────────────> ENDED
    23→ENDED ──git commit─────────────────> ENDED + CONDENSE (remaining data)
    24→ENDED ──resume (UserPromptSubmit)──> ACTIVE (clear EndedAt)
    25→TIMED_OUT (virtual): LastInteractionTime > TTL, treated as ENDED
    26→```
    27→
    28→### Timeout Detection
    29→
    30→**Tiered TTL:** Different contexts use different staleness thresholds:
    31→- **Post-commit hook**: 2-minute TTL. Agents interact frequently (tool calls every few seconds), so 2 min of silence strongly indicates the session is dead or interrupted. Fast recovery for Ctrl-C/kill scenarios.
    32→- **General cleanup** (UserPromptSubmit scanning other sessions): 30-minute TTL. More conservative since it's housekeeping.
    33→
    34→### Known Limitation: False-Positive Timeout on Permission Prompts
    35→
    36→If the agent is alive but blocked on a permission prompt for > 2 minutes and the user commits in another terminal, post-commit falsely treats the session as TIMED_OUT. Impact: premature condensation + shadow branch deletion. When the agent continues, SaveChanges creates a new shadow branch — session data is split across two condensations. Not data loss, just two entries instead of one. Acceptable given the rarity of this case.
    37→
    38→### Known Limitation: Ctrl-C Interruption
    39→
    40→When a user Ctrl-C's during agent work, Claude Code shows "Interrupted - What should Claude do instead?" — the agent is alive at the prompt, but no hook fires. The session stays in ACTIVE even though the agent is idle.
    41→
    42→- If user types another prompt → UserPromptSubmit detects ACTIVE→ACTIVE transition → **catch-up checkpoint** captures the interrupted turn's data → continues normally
    43→- If user commits immediately (no new prompt first) → post-commit sees ACTIVE with recent LastInteractionTime → skips condensation. **Data is safe** on shadow branch, just won't condense on that commit. Will condense on next commit after Stop fires, session ends, or TTL expires.
    44→- Potential future fix: Claude Code adds an "interrupted" hook (track upstream)
    45→
    46→Key behaviors:
    47→- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch
    48→- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)
    49→- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed
    50→- **All sessions included in condensation**: Even sessions with no file changes get transcript captured
    51→- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session
    52→
    53→## Implementation Steps
    54→
    55→### Step 1: File Locking on StateStore
    56→
    57→Add `flock`-based `LoadModifySave()` to `session.StateStore` for atomic read-modify-write.
    58→
    59→**Files:**
    60→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(*State) error) error`
    61→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Concurrent access tests, timeout tests, error propagation tests
    62→
    63→**Design:**
    64→```go
    65→func (s *StateStore) LoadModifySave(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, fn func(*State) error) error {
    66→    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + ".lock"
    67→    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
    68→    defer f.Close()
    69→    defer os.Remove(lockPath)
    70→    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired
    71→    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)
    72→
    73→    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
    74→    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }
    75→    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }
    76→    return s.Save(ctx, state)
    77→}
    78→```
    79→
    80→- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only operations (listing, status)
    81→- `LoadModifySave` is the only way to mutate state safely

   118→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`: use `LoadModifySave`, update LastInteractionTime. Check if Phase==ACTIVE_COMMITTED and trigger condensation on Stop transition.
   119→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `commitWithMetadata()` (Stop handler): after SaveChanges, check if phase was ACTIVE_COMMITTED and condense if so. Transition phase to IDLE.
   120→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `markSessionEnded()`: use `LoadModifySave`, set Phase=ENDED
   121→
   122→**InitializeSession changes:**

   124→- ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED → ACTIVE: Previous turn was interrupted (Ctrl-C, crash). **Catch-up checkpoint**: read live transcript from `CondensedTranscriptLines`/`TranscriptLinesAtStart`, create shadow branch checkpoint for the interrupted turn's data. Then reset to ACTIVE for new turn. This ensures interrupted turns don't lose transcript data.
   125→
   126→**Stop handler changes (commitWithMetadata):**
