    47→When a user Ctrl-C's during agent work, Claude Code shows "Interrupted - What should Claude do instead?" — the agent is alive at the prompt, but no hook fires. The session stays in ACTIVE even though the agent is idle.
    48→
    49→- If user types another prompt → UserPromptSubmit detects ACTIVE→ACTIVE transition → **catch-up checkpoint** captures the interrupted turn's data → continues normally
    50→- If user commits immediately (no new prompt first) → post-commit sees ACTIVE with recent LastInteractionTime → skips condensation. **Data is safe** on shadow branch, just won't condense on that commit. Will condense on next commit after Stop fires, session ends, or TTL expires.
    51→- Potential future fix: Claude Code adds an "interrupted" hook (track upstream)
    52→
    53→Key behaviors:
    54→- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch
    55→- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)
    56→- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed or discarded
    57→- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session
    58→
    59→### Condensation Eligibility
    60→
    61→Not all sessions are condensed. Ended sessions that made no file contributions are discarded:
    62→
    63→| Phase | FilesTouched? | Action |
    64→|---|---|---|
    65→| ACTIVE / IDLE | Any | **Condense** (session ongoing, context valuable) |
    66→| ENDED / TIMED_OUT | Non-empty | **Condense** (contributed to code) |
    67→| ENDED / TIMED_OUT | Empty | **Discard** (no code contribution, noise) |
    68→
    69→**Simplified rule:** Discard only when `(ENDED or TIMED_OUT) AND len(FilesTouched) == 0`.
    70→
    71→Additionally, users can explicitly remove a session from checkpointing via `entire reset --session <id>`. This clears the session state; any file changes remain in the working directory for the user to handle via git.
    72→
    73→## PR Sequencing
    74→
    75→```
    76→PR 1: File Locking (foundational, no behavior change)
    77→  → Steps 1 + 6, with unit tests
    78→
    79→PR 2: Phase Field + State Machine Core (state tracking, no git hook changes)
    80→  → Steps 2 + 3, with unit + integration tests for phase transitions
    81→
    82→PR 3: Phase-Aware Git Hooks (the actual bug fix)
    83→  → Steps 4 + 5, with integration tests for mid-session commits
    84→
    85→PR 4: Cleanup + Polish
    86→  → Steps 7 + 8 + 9 + 10, with remaining tests + docs
    87→```
    88→
    89→Each PR ships with its own tests. PRs are independently reviewable and deployable in order.
    90→
    91→---
    92→
    93→## Implementation Steps
    94→
    95→### Step 1: File Locking on StateStore (PR 1)
    96→
    97→Add `flock`-based `LoadModifySave()` to `session.StateStore` for atomic read-modify-write.
    98→
    99→**Files:**
   100→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(*State) error) error`
   101→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Concurrent access tests, timeout tests, error propagation tests
   102→
   103→**Design:**
   104→```go
   105→func (s *StateStore) LoadModifySave(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, fn func(*State) error) error {
   106→    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + ".lock"
   107→    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
   108→    defer f.Close()
   109→    defer os.Remove(lockPath)
   110→    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired
   111→    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)
   112→
   113→    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   114→    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }
   115→    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }
   116→    return s.Save(ctx, state)
   117→}
   118→```
   119→
   120→- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only operations (listing, status)
   121→- `LoadModifySave` is the only way to mutate state safely
   122→
   123→### Step 2: Phase Field on Session State (PR 2)
   124→
   125→Add `Phase` enum and `PendingCheckpointID` to both `session.State` and `strategy.SessionState`.
   126→
   127→**Files:**
   128→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `Phase` type + constants, `PendingCheckpointID` field, helper methods (`IsActive()`, `CanCondense()`, `IsTimedOut()`)
   129→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_types.go` — Mirror `Phase` and `PendingCheckpointID` fields on `SessionState`
   130→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit.go` — Update `sessionStateToStrategy()` and `sessionStateFromStrategy()` conversion functions
   131→- `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Phase transition tests, backward compat (missing phase = IDLE)
   132→
   133→**New types in `session/state.go`:**
   134→```go
   135→type Phase string
   136→const (
   137→    PhaseActive          Phase = "active"
   138→    PhaseActiveCommitted Phase = "active_committed"
   139→    PhaseIdle            Phase = "idle"
   140→    PhaseEnded           Phase = "ended"
   141→)
   142→```
   143→
   144→**New fields on `State`:**
   145→```go
   146→Phase               Phase           `json:"phase,omitempty"`             // empty = idle (backward compat)
   147→PendingCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
   148→```
   149→
   150→**Backward compat:** Missing/empty Phase treated as `PhaseIdle`.
   151→
   152→### Step 3: Phase Transitions in Agent Hooks (PR 2)
   153→
   154→Wire up `SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`, and `SessionStop` to set phases using `LoadModifySave`.
   155→
   156→**Files:**
   157→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks.go` — `handleSessionStartCommon()`: check for existing state file, transition ENDED → IDLE on re-entry
   158→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `InitializeSession()`: set Phase=ACTIVE, clear EndedAt on resume, update LastInteractionTime
   159→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`: use `LoadModifySave`, update LastInteractionTime. Check if Phase==ACTIVE_COMMITTED and trigger condensation on Stop transition.
   160→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `commitWithMetadata()` (Stop handler): after SaveChanges, check if phase was ACTIVE_COMMITTED and condense if so. Transition phase to IDLE.
   161→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — `markSessionEnded()`: use `LoadModifySave`, set Phase=ENDED
   162→
   163→**InitializeSession changes:**
   164→- IDLE/ENDED → ACTIVE: Set phase, clear EndedAt, generate PendingCheckpointID if missing
   165→- ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED → ACTIVE: Previous turn was interrupted (Ctrl-C, crash). **Catch-up checkpoint**: read live transcript from `CondensedTranscriptLines`/`TranscriptLinesAtStart`, create shadow branch checkpoint for the interrupted turn's data. Then reset to ACTIVE for new turn. This ensures interrupted turns don't lose transcript data.
   166→
   167→**Stop handler changes (commitWithMetadata):**
   168→- Load state, check phase BEFORE calling SaveChanges
   169→- After SaveChanges:
   170→  - If was ACTIVE_COMMITTED → condense immediately, set phase=IDLE
   171→  - If was ACTIVE → set phase=IDLE (normal, condense on user commit)
   172→
   173→### Step 4: Phase-Aware PostCommit (PR 3)
   174→
   175→Update `PostCommit()` to check phase and defer condensation for active sessions.
   176→
   177→**Files:**
   178→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PostCommit()`: for ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED sessions, skip condensation, update BaseCommit + migrate shadow branch, set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   179→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_migration.go` — Ensure `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded` works when called from PostCommit context
   180→
   181→**PostCommit logic per session:**
   182→```
   183→if phase == ACTIVE:
   184→    phase = ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   185→    migrate shadow branch to new HEAD
   186→    update BaseCommit
   187→    SKIP condensation
   188→
   189→if phase == ACTIVE_COMMITTED:
   190→    migrate shadow branch to new HEAD
   191→    update BaseCommit
   192→    SKIP condensation
   193→
   194→if phase == IDLE:
   195→    condense (existing behavior)
   196→
   197→if phase == ENDED or TIMED_OUT:
   198→    if len(FilesTouched) > 0 → condense (session contributed code)
   199→    if len(FilesTouched) == 0 → discard (no code contribution, clear state)
   200→```
   201→
   202→**Shadow branch deletion:**
   203→```
   204→canDelete = true
   205→for each session on this shadow branch:
   206→    if session.Phase == ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED:
   207→        canDelete = false
   208→if canDelete:
   209→    delete shadow branch
   210→```
   211→
   212→### Step 5: Phase-Aware PrepareCommitMsg (PR 3)
   213→
   214→Update `PrepareCommitMsg()` to use `PendingCheckpointID` from session state.
   215→
   216→**Files:**
   217→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PrepareCommitMsg()`: use existing PendingCheckpointID from state instead of generating new one each time
   218→
   219→**Logic:**
   220→- If any session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID: use it
   221→- If no PendingCheckpointID exists: generate one, store in state via LoadModifySave
   222→- All commits in a session share the same PendingCheckpointID
   223→
   224→### Step 6: Migrate Locking to All Call Sites (PR 1)
   225→
   226→Replace bare load+save patterns with `LoadModifySave` across all mutation sites.
   227→
   228→**Files:**
   229→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_git.go` — `SaveChanges()`, `SaveTaskCheckpoint()`
   230→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `PostCommit()`, `InitializeSession()`
   231→- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — Add `loadModifySaveSessionState()` wrapper
   232→
   233→**Approach:** The strategy layer calls `stateStore.LoadModifySave()` via a helper that handles the `session.State` ↔ `strategy.SessionState` conversion:
   234→
   235→```go
   236→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadModifySaveSessionState(
   237→    sessionID string, fn func(*SessionState) error,
   238→) error {
   239→    store, _ := s.getStateStore()
   240→    return store.LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(state *session.State) error {
   241→        stratState := sessionStateToStrategy(state)
   242→        if err := fn(stratState); err != nil { return err }
   243→        *state = *sessionStateFromStrategy(stratState)
   244→        return nil
   245→    })
   246→}
   247→```
   248→

   255→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_geminicli_handlers.go:48` — Remove `paths.ReadCurrentSession()` usage (get session ID from hook input instead)
   256→- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_git_cmd.go:67` — `initHookLogging()`: get session ID from session state files instead of current_session file
   257→- `cmd/entire/cli/debug.go:126` — `debugShowCommand`: list active sessions from StateStore instead

   264→

   266→

   270→
   271→**Logic:**
   272→```
   273→entire reset --session <id>:
   274→  1. Load session state by ID
   275→  2. Clear session state file
   276→  3. If no other sessions reference the shadow branch → delete shadow branch
   277→  4. If other sessions still need it → leave shadow branch
   278→  5. File changes remain in working directory (user handles via git)

   290→2. Stop after mid-session commit: verify condensation happens at Stop
   291→3. Multiple mid-session commits: verify single condensation at Stop
   292→4. Concurrent sessions: one commits mid-session, verify independent phases
   293→5. Session resume: ENDED → ACTIVE clears EndedAt
   294→6. Shadow branch preserved: verify shadow branch NOT deleted while ACTIVE session exists
   295→7. `entire reset` with ACTIVE session: verify warning/refusal

   305→
   306→## Edge Cases: External Git Operations
   307→
   308→### `entire reset` while session is active
   309→
   310→`entire reset` deletes the shadow branch and all session state files for the current HEAD. If called while a session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED:
   311→- Agent's subsequent SaveChanges will fail to find state, falls back to `initializeSession()` (self-heals)
   312→- But all previous checkpoint data is lost
   313→
   314→**Action (Step 4):** Add guard in `Reset()` — if any session on the shadow branch has phase ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED, warn and require `--force`.
   315→
   316→### `git stash` while IDLE
   317→
   318→- HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire
   319→- Shadow branch + session state untouched
   320→- `git stash pop` restores files
   321→- **Safe.** No changes needed.
   322→
   323→### `git reset --hard` (to HEAD) while IDLE
   324→
   325→- Cleans working directory, HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire
   326→- Agent's changes gone from working directory but **preserved on shadow branch**
   327→- User can `entire rewind` to restore
   328→- **Safe.** No changes needed.
   329→
   330→### `git reset --hard <older-commit>` while IDLE
   331→
   332→- HEAD moves backward, no hooks fire
   333→- Session state still has old BaseCommit
   334→- On next `UserPromptSubmit` → `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` renames shadow branch
   335→- **Collision risk:** if the older commit already has a shadow branch, it gets overwritten
   336→- This is a pre-existing issue, not introduced by our changes
   337→- **Out of scope** for this PR, but noted for future hardening
   338→
   339→### `git rebase` while ACTIVE
   340→
   341→`prepare-commit-msg` already skips during sequence operations (rebase, cherry-pick, revert) — no trailer added. `post-commit` fires for each rebased commit: sees ACTIVE → transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrates shadow branch. After rebase, shadow branch is at the new HEAD. `git rebase --abort` resets HEAD; next UserPromptSubmit migrates back. **Handled**, but many rapid migrations could be noisy (logging only, not user-visible).
   342→
   343→### `git reset --hard` while ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   344→
   345→- HEAD moves but agent is still running
   346→- Agent may do another commit → post-commit fires
   347→- Our new post-commit (Step 4) calls `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` which handles the HEAD change
   348→- **Handled** by the migration in post-commit.
   349→
   350→### User abandons work (git restore) then starts new session
   351→
   352→User works with Claude, creates files, then does `git restore .` and either closes Claude first (ENDED) or opens new Claude alongside (concurrent sessions).
   353→
   354→- Old session's `FilesTouched` still lists files (even though reverted)
   355→- If ENDED + FilesTouched non-empty → condensed with historical data (accurate record of what happened)
   356→- If user wants to fully discard → use `entire reset --session <id>`
   357→- New session starts fresh, shares shadow branch, no cross-contamination in condensation (filtered by session ID trailer)
   358→- Attribution is per-session, so abandoned session's stats don't affect active session
   359→
   360→## Coordination
   361→
   362→- **PR #165 (LastInteractionTime)**: This plan uses LastInteractionTime for crash/timeout detection. If PR #165 hasn't landed, we add the field ourselves. If it has, we reuse it.
   363→- **entire/sessions → entire/checkpoints/v1 rename**: Account for the branch rename in any new code.
   364→
   365→## Verification
   366→
   367→After implementation:
   368→```bash
   369→mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
   370→```
   371→
   372→Manual verification:
   373→1. Start a session, have agent commit mid-session, verify no condensation
   374→2. Stop the session, verify condensation happens
   375→3. Commit normally, verify trailer links to correct checkpoint
   376→4. Check `entire status` shows correct phase info
   377→5. Kill a session mid-work, verify timeout detection works on next commit
   378→

  "content": "# ENT-221 + ENT-194: Session Phase State Machine\n\n> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.\n\n**Goal:** Introduce a session phase state machine so hooks can distinguish \"agent committed mid-session\" from \"user committed between sessions\", preventing premature condensation and data loss.\n\n**Architecture:** Add a Phase field to session state that tracks the session lifecycle (ACTIVE, ACTIVE_COMMITTED, IDLE, ENDED). Git hooks check the phase before deciding whether to condense. File locking prevents concurrent hook processes from corrupting state.\n\n**Tech Stack:** Go, go-git v5, syscall.Flock\n\n---\n\n## Context\n\nWhen an AI agent commits via Bash mid-session (between UserPromptSubmit and Stop hooks), `post-commit` fires and prematurely condenses session data, resets checkpoint count, and deletes the shadow branch. This causes:\n\n- **ENT-194**: Sequential agent commits produce empty/incomplete checkpoints because each `post-commit` condenses before the transcript is complete\n- **ENT-221**: No cross-hook state tracking means hooks can't distinguish \"agent committed mid-session\" from \"user committed between sessions\"\n\nThe fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.\n\n## State Machine\n\n```\nUNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──────> ACTIVE  (state file created)\nACTIVE ──Stop─────────────────────────> IDLE\nACTIVE ──git commit───────────────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED\nACTIVE_COMMITTED ──git commit─────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED\nACTIVE_COMMITTED ──Stop───────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE (immediate)\nIDLE ──git commit─────────────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE\nIDLE ──UserPromptSubmit───────────────> ACTIVE\nIDLE ──SessionStop────────────────────> ENDED\nENDED ──SessionStart (re-entry)───────> IDLE  (clear EndedAt, immediately condensable)\nENDED ──git commit────────────────────> ENDED + CONDENSE (if FilesTouched non-empty)\nIDLE/ENDED ──UserPromptSubmit─────────> ACTIVE (clear EndedAt if needed)\nTIMED_OUT (virtual): LastInteractionTime > TTL, treated as ENDED\n```\n\n**SessionStart re-entry:** When `SessionStart` fires and a state file exists:\n- Phase = ENDED → set Phase = IDLE, clear EndedAt (session re-entered, immediately condensable)\n- Phase = IDLE → no-op (already condensable)\n- Phase = ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED → leave as-is (stale session, will timeout or catch-up)\n- No state file → UNINITIALIZED (wait for UserPromptSubmit to create state)\n\n### Key Behaviors\n\n- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch\n- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)\n- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed or discarded\n- **All sessions included in condensation**: Even sessions with no file changes get transcript captured\n- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session\n\n### Timeout Detection\n\n**Tiered TTL:** Different contexts use different staleness thresholds:\n- **Post-commit hook**: 2-minute TTL. Agents interact frequently (tool calls every few seconds), so 2 min of silence strongly indicates the session is dead or interrupted. Fast recovery for Ctrl-C/kill scenarios.\n- **General cleanup** (UserPromptSubmit scanning other sessions): 30-minute TTL. More conservative since it's housekeeping.\n\n### Condensation Eligibility\n\nNot all sessions are condensed. Ended sessions that made no file contributions are discarded:\n\n| Phase | FilesTouched? | Action |\n|---|---|---|\n| ACTIVE / IDLE | Any | **Condense** (session ongoing, context valuable) |\n| ENDED / TIMED_OUT | Non-empty | **Condense** (contributed to code) |\n| ENDED / TIMED_OUT | Empty | **Discard** (no code contribution, noise) |\n\n**Simplified rule:** Discard only when `(ENDED or TIMED_OUT) AND len(FilesTouched) == 0`.\n\nAdditionally, users can explicitly remove a session from checkpointing via `entire reset --session <id>`. This clears the session state; any file changes remain in the working directory for the user to handle via git.\n\n### Known Limitations\n\n**False-positive timeout on permission prompts:** If the agent is alive but blocked on a permission prompt for > 2 minutes and the user commits in another terminal, post-commit falsely treats the session as TIMED_OUT. Impact: premature condensation + shadow branch deletion. When the agent continues, SaveChanges creates a new shadow branch — session data is split across two condensations. Not data loss, just two entries instead of one.\n\n**Ctrl-C interruption:** When a user Ctrl-C's during agent work, Claude Code shows \"Interrupted - What should Claude do instead?\" but no hook fires. The session stays in ACTIVE.\n- If user types another prompt → UserPromptSubmit detects ACTIVE→ACTIVE transition → **catch-up checkpoint** captures the interrupted turn's data → continues normally\n- If user commits immediately (no new prompt first) → post-commit sees ACTIVE with recent LastInteractionTime → skips condensation. Data is safe on shadow branch, condenses later.\n- Potential future fix: Claude Code adds an \"interrupted\" hook (track upstream)\n\n---\n\n## PR Sequencing\n\n```\nPR 1: File Locking (foundational, no behavior change)\n  → Steps 1 + 6, with unit tests\n\nPR 2: Phase Field + State Machine Core (state tracking, no git hook changes)\n  → Steps 2 + 3, with unit + integration tests for phase transitions\n\nPR 3: Phase-Aware Git Hooks (the actual bug fix)\n  → Steps 4 + 5, with integration tests for mid-session commits\n\nPR 4: Cleanup + Polish\n  → Steps 7 + 8 + 9 + 10, with remaining tests + docs\n```\n\nEach PR ships with its own tests. The file /Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/1/docs/plans/2026-02-06-session-phase-state-machine.md has been updated. Here's the result of running `cat -n` on a snippet of the edited file:
     1→# ENT-221 + ENT-194: Session Phase State Machine
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Introduce a session phase state machine so hooks can distinguish "agent committed mid-session" from "user committed between sessions", preventing premature condensation and data loss.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Add a Phase field to session state that tracks the session lifecycle (ACTIVE, ACTIVE_COMMITTED, IDLE, ENDED). Git hooks check the phase before deciding whether to condense. File locking prevents concurrent hook processes from corrupting state.
     8→
     9→**Tech Stack:** Go, go-git v5, syscall.Flock
    10→
    11→---
    12→
    13→## Context
    14→
    15→When an AI agent commits via Bash mid-session (between UserPromptSubmit and Stop hooks), `post-commit` fires and prematurely condenses session data, resets checkpoint count, and deletes the shadow branch. This causes:
    16→
    17→- **ENT-194**: Sequential agent commits produce empty/incomplete checkpoints because each `post-commit` condenses before the transcript is complete
    18→- **ENT-221**: No cross-hook state tracking means hooks can't distinguish "agent committed mid-session" from "user committed between sessions"
    19→
    20→The fix introduces a **session phase state machine** so hooks know the session's lifecycle stage, plus **file locking** to prevent concurrent hook processes from corrupting session state.
    21→
    22→## State Machine
    23→
    24→```
    25→UNINITIALIZED ──UserPromptSubmit──────> ACTIVE  (state file created)
    26→ACTIVE ──Stop─────────────────────────> IDLE
    27→ACTIVE ──git commit───────────────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    28→ACTIVE_COMMITTED ──git commit─────────> ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    29→ACTIVE_COMMITTED ──Stop───────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE (immediate)
    30→IDLE ──git commit─────────────────────> IDLE + CONDENSE
    31→IDLE ──UserPromptSubmit───────────────> ACTIVE
    32→IDLE ──SessionStop────────────────────> ENDED
    33→ENDED ──SessionStart (re-entry)───────> IDLE  (clear EndedAt, immediately condensable)
    34→ENDED ──git commit────────────────────> ENDED + CONDENSE (if FilesTouched non-empty)
    35→IDLE/ENDED ──UserPromptSubmit─────────> ACTIVE (clear EndedAt if needed)
    36→TIMED_OUT (virtual): LastInteractionTime > TTL, treated as ENDED
    37→```
    38→
    39→**SessionStart re-entry:** When `SessionStart` fires and a state file exists:
    40→- Phase = ENDED → set Phase = IDLE, clear EndedAt (session re-entered, immediately condensable)
    41→- Phase = IDLE → no-op (already condensable)
    42→- Phase = ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED → leave as-is (stale session, will timeout or catch-up)
    43→- No state file → UNINITIALIZED (wait for UserPromptSubmit to create state)
    44→
    45→### Key Behaviors
    46→
    47→- **ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED**: `post-commit` defers condensation, only updates BaseCommit + migrates shadow branch
    48→- **ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE**: Stop triggers immediate condensation (agent's commits already exist on branch)
    49→- **Shadow branch deletion**: Only when ALL sessions on the branch are condensed or discarded
    50→- **All sessions included in condensation**: Even sessions with no file changes get transcript captured
    51→- **PendingCheckpointID**: Generated once, shared across all commits in a session
    52→
    53→### Timeout Detection
    54→
    55→**Tiered TTL:** Different contexts use different staleness thresholds:
    56→- **Post-commit hook**: 2-minute TTL. Agents interact frequently (tool calls every few seconds), so 2 min of silence strongly indicates the session is dead or interrupted. Fast recovery for Ctrl-C/kill scenarios.
    57→- **General cleanup** (UserPromptSubmit scanning other sessions): 30-minute TTL. More conservative since it's housekeeping.
    58→
    59→### Condensation Eligibility
    60→
    61→Not all sessions are condensed. Ended sessions that made no file contributions are discarded:
    62→
    63→| Phase | FilesTouched? | Action |
    64→|---|---|---|
    65→| ACTIVE / IDLE | Any | **Condense** (session ongoing, context valuable) |
    66→| ENDED / TIMED_OUT | Non-empty | **Condense** (contributed to code) |
    67→| ENDED / TIMED_OUT | Empty | **Discard** (no code contribution, noise) |
    68→
    69→**Simplified rule:** Discard only when `(ENDED or TIMED_OUT) AND len(FilesTouched) == 0`.
    70→
    71→Additionally, users can explicitly remove a session from checkpointing via `entire reset --session <id>`. This clears the session state; any file changes remain in the working directory for the user to handle via git.
    72→
    73→### Known Limitations
    74→
    75→**False-positive timeout on permission prompts:** If the agent is alive but blocked on a permission prompt for > 2 minutes and the user commits in another terminal, post-commit falsely treats the session as TIMED_OUT. Impact: premature condensation + shadow branch deletion. When the agent continues, SaveChanges creates a new shadow branch — session data is split across two condensations. Not data loss, just two entries instead of one.
    76→
    77→**Ctrl-C interruption:** When a user Ctrl-C's during agent work, Claude Code shows "Interrupted - What should Claude do instead?" but no hook fires. The session stays in ACTIVE.
    78→- If user types another prompt → UserPromptSubmit detects ACTIVE→ACTIVE transition → **catch-up checkpoint** captures the interrupted turn's data → continues normally
    79→- If user commits immediately (no new prompt first) → post-commit sees ACTIVE with recent LastInteractionTime → skips condensation. Data is safe on shadow branch, condenses later.
    80→- Potential future fix: Claude Code adds an "interrupted" hook (track upstream)
    81→
    82→---
    83→
    84→## PR Sequencing
    85→
    86→```
    87→PR 1: File Locking (foundational, no behavior change)
    88→  → Steps 1 + 6, with unit tests
    89→
    90→PR 2: Phase Field + State Machine Core (state tracking, no git hook changes)
    91→  → Steps 2 + 3, with unit + integration tests for phase transitions
    92→
    93→PR 3: Phase-Aware Git Hooks (the actual bug fix)
    94→  → Steps 4 + 5, with integration tests for mid-session commits
    95→
    96→PR 4: Cleanup + Polish
    97→  → Steps 7 + 8 + 9 + 10, with remaining tests + docs
    98→```
    99→
   100→Each PR ships with its own tests. PRs are independently reviewable and deployable in order.

   103→
   104→## Task 1: File Locking on StateStore (PR 1)
   105→
   106→Add `flock`-based `LoadModifySave()` to `session.StateStore` for atomic read-modify-write.
   107→
   108→**Files:**
   109→- Modify: `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` — Add `LoadModifySave(ctx, sessionID, func(*State) error) error`
   110→- Modify: `cmd/entire/cli/session/state_test.go` — Concurrent access tests, timeout tests, error propagation tests
   111→
   112→**Design:**
   113→```go
   114→func (s *StateStore) LoadModifySave(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, fn func(*State) error) error {
   115→    lockPath := s.stateFilePath(sessionID) + ".lock"
   116→    f, _ := os.OpenFile(lockPath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
   117→    defer f.Close()
   118→    defer os.Remove(lockPath)
   119→    syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_EX) // blocks until acquired
   120→    defer syscall.Flock(int(f.Fd()), syscall.LOCK_UN)
   121→
   122→    state, _ := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   123→    if state == nil { state = &State{SessionID: sessionID} }
   124→    if err := fn(state); err != nil { return err }
   125→    return s.Save(ctx, state)
   126→}
   127→```
   128→
   129→- Keep existing `Load()` unlocked for read-only operations (listing, status)
   130→- `LoadModifySave` is the only way to mutate state safely
   131→
   132→**Verification:** `mise run test` — new unit tests pass

   308→## Task 8: Targeted Session Reset (PR 4)
   309→
   310→Add `--session` flag to `entire reset` for per-session cleanup.
   311→
   312→**Files:**
   313→- Modify: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_reset.go` — Add per-session reset logic
   314→- Modify: `cmd/entire/cli/commands/reset.go` (or wherever reset command lives) — Add `--session` flag
   315→
   316→**Logic:**
   317→```
   318→entire reset --session <id>:
   319→  1. Load session state by ID
   320→  2. Clear session state file
   321→  3. If no other sessions reference the shadow branch → delete shadow branch
   322→  4. If other sessions still need it → leave shadow branch
   323→  5. File changes remain in working directory (user handles via git)
   324→```
   325→
   326→Also update the existing `entire reset` (no args) to check for ACTIVE sessions and warn/require `--force`.
   327→
   328→**Verification:** `mise run test:ci`

   362→
   363→## Edge Cases: External Git Operations
   364→
   365→### `entire reset` while session is active
   366→
   367→`entire reset` deletes the shadow branch and all session state files for the current HEAD. If called while a session is ACTIVE/ACTIVE_COMMITTED, all previous checkpoint data is lost (agent self-heals via `initializeSession()` fallback).
   368→
   369→**Action (Task 4):** Add guard in `Reset()` — if any session has phase ACTIVE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED, warn and require `--force`.
   370→
   371→### `git stash` while IDLE
   372→
   373→HEAD unchanged, no hooks fire. Shadow branch + session state untouched. `git stash pop` restores files. **Safe.**
   374→
   375→### `git reset --hard` (to HEAD) while IDLE
   376→
   377→Cleans working directory, HEAD unchanged. Agent's changes gone from working directory but **preserved on shadow branch**. User can `entire rewind` to restore. **Safe.**
   378→
   379→### `git reset --hard <older-commit>` while IDLE
   380→
   381→HEAD moves backward, no hooks fire. On next `UserPromptSubmit` → `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` renames shadow branch. **Collision risk** if older commit already has a shadow branch (pre-existing issue, out of scope).
   382→
   383→### `git rebase` while ACTIVE
   384→
   385→`prepare-commit-msg` skips during sequence operations — no trailer added. `post-commit` fires for each rebased commit: sees ACTIVE → transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrates shadow branch. After rebase, shadow branch is at new HEAD. **Handled.**
   386→
   387→### `git reset --hard` while ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   388→
   389→HEAD moves, agent still running. Our new post-commit (Task 4) calls `migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded()` which handles the HEAD change. **Handled.**
   390→
   391→### User abandons work (git restore) then starts new session
   392→
   393→User works with Claude, creates files, then does `git restore .` and either closes Claude first (ENDED) or opens new Claude alongside (concurrent sessions).
   394→
   395→- Old session's `FilesTouched` still lists files (even though reverted)
   396→- If ENDED + FilesTouched non-empty → condensed with historical data (accurate record of what happened)
   397→- If user wants to fully discard → use `entire reset --session <id>`
   398→- New session starts fresh, shares shadow branch, no cross-contamination in condensation (filtered by session ID trailer)
   399→- Attribution is per-session, so abandoned session's stats don't affect active session
   400→
   401→---
